- **2026-11-11** | [[Ordered Pair]] (Pre-Algebra) | L4 Wield, L5 Command | Source: own | full text in Session.md "Quiz: Ordered Pair" (copied here)
  - L4: (a) $\Phi(((x,y),z))=(x,(y,z))$ is a well-defined bijection $(X\times Y)\times Z\to X\times(Y\times Z)$ (say what well-definedness requires); (b) with $X=Y=Z=\{\varnothing\}$ and Kuratowski pairs, the two products are disjoint. Exercises [[Cartesian Product]], [[Well-Defined Function]], [[Bijective Function]].
  - L5: (a) the naive triple $\{\{a\},\{a,b\},\{a,b,c\}\}$ fails the characteristic property; which feature of the pair proof breaks? (b) canonical bijection $A\times B\cong\prod_{i\in\{1,2\}}X_i$ commuting with projections; why Def. 3 has no bracketing issue and why it, not iterated pairing, extends to $\mathbb{R}^{\mathbb{N}}$. Exercises [[Cartesian Product]] Def. 3, [[Bijective Function]].
